<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">January is about preparing marketers for brand building in 2026. This week is about Gen-Z, next one will be out about….</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you’ll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7f221a41-b15d-4e94-affb-55bebe989797?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">The Obvious</h3>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong><span>Gen-Zers are facing a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a69ddc1a-19b4-4500-9ab4-262042b7252e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">male loneliness epidemic</a><span>. Are they?</span></strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Male loneliness epidemic </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bfac76a6-1364-4308-b18d-615f4a427eb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">misses</a><span> women struggling in US.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The men’s loneliness epidemic </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/849116a1-3473-437a-8415-79cb60256ba4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">might not</a><span> exist.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Stat vs. Stat: In the US, two-thirds of men aged </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5711e354-a486-42d7-bb2d-cfe610838933?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">between 18 and 23</a><span> think that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dbbf3feb-0723-4628-8850-bc22e62dcf2c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">nobody really knows them</a><span>; one third </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8668632d-c667-42d6-8252-76e3e0cdc626?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">haven’t seen anyone</a><span> outside their household in the past week; only a</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8668632d-c667-42d6-8252-76e3e0cdc626?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> </a><span>fifth say they have friends they can really count on; and a staggering </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6a4b08fe-303d-4a76-bf7f-5b94d2a66c6b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">69% of young men</a><span> think “no one cares if men are OK”.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-roman;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>People in poor countries are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13a6feb4-0db3-4c55-b428-68f0eac9c8a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">much likelier to be lonely</a><span>. And the loneliest region of all, surprisingly, is Africa, the home of ubuntu, the sociable notion that “people are people through other people.” In 2024 over a quarter of Africans surveyed said they had felt lonely the previous day.</span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">If you ask me, the debate around loneliness, conscientiousness, and the evils of social media hinges on the variety of questions being asked in surveys. It seems like we keep looking for more evidence to debate what we should change, rather than actually changing something to make people care about each other.</p></li>
</ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5028c593-af5d-4799-bbb7-3609c33f2635?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen-Z</a><span> will go </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/56e9ff75-0ac0-4886-a5a2-6680e95bab50?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">analog</a><span> or </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/965baa34-f702-49e3-8d34-278ff0360ea5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">friction-maxxing</a><span> this year.</span></strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>No, they won’t. The media is confusing Gen Z having a crashout over the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/52796811-f656-4400-acaf-1384c0f1dd0c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">enshittification</a><span> of digital platforms with their love and nostalgia for old gadgets.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>People are opting for older technologies to solve specific problems, like a movie title constantly moving from one platform to another, people losing their AirPods, bad audio on some Bluetooth devices, annoying Windows updates, streaming price increases, and more. On top of this, we have evidence that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d7c24e77-9fd7-43e4-b4d5-dd8661b7cbd2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">digital screens in modern cars</a><span> actually decrease driver focus, so people want screenless car interiors.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>We won’t see anything broadly different from what we saw this year. There are always old-tech trends that use nostalgia to lure in Gen Z for a while, and then they bounce back to new tech after a few Instagram dumps. There are also instances like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/da718957-fb03-4b1e-850d-a2908c596132?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fandoms</a><span> of Friends and The Office buying DVDs because streaming platforms keep trading the rights.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Almost forgot, the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/35096678-5e6f-4c75-9edc-e231b4f0ad0e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">expansion of phone bans</a><span> in schools and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a53068f5-1d39-4986-8788-5eae319dfbbd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">social media age restriction</a><span> could lead Gen Z and Gen Alpha toward older technologies, and they might actually embrace them long term.</span></p></li>
</ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong><span>Does Gen Z still </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/533262b0-f17e-4662-a7c5-0f82d1790395?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prefers</a><span> in-person shopping?</span></strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>22% </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0da614f5-6408-4485-b008-5341b5dce3cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prefer</a><span> shopping in-store for clothing (vs. 16% mostly online).</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Nearly 24% of Gen Zers chose to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cfe7a6c8-50a0-4c10-a6aa-e9322c99df30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">thrift or DIY</a><span> their home decor. Others (40%) opted to blend new with secondhand. Even with apparel, 23% shopped secondhand, while 35% mixed thrifted with new clothing.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-roman;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>One reason is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4b9bbf6f-5576-4cac-a854-60bce4279ef9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">money</a><span>. Gen Z-ers’ holiday spending this year is expected to fall 23 percent, according to recent research, so a used sweater from a big brand that costs less and lasts longer feels like a win.</span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e092c75b-5ea9-403d-bd2f-37a8465f6edb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Malls</a><span> are popular with zoomers, but they show less brand loyalty and are happy to settle for ‘dupes’ over the real thing.</span></p></li>
</ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>Gen-Z wants authenticity from brands and possibly everyone. </strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>When asked how important various brand behaviours are, 62 % of Gen-Z say </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b3d45aac-73c8-4f3b-ad78-69ba5dec4390?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">honesty</a><span> is “very important,” followed by trustworthiness at 61 %, and consistency between what brands say and do at 56 %.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>What’s authentic when you can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ae51eb7e-bb7c-4c3a-8760-8ff5b6f15093?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">easily</a><span> use AI to generate an aesthetic that would be considered “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b7ea6734-7e28-41fd-8be7-9fa207931edd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">gen-z influencer</a><span>”? </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Don’t waste time trying to manufacture authenticity, you are destined to fail, as the majority of Gen Zers who grew up with social media consistently redefine what’s authentic and what’s performative. Stick to what you believe is authentic to your brand and character.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Also, authenticity in marketing is such a vague word. Marketing is all about performance. Authenticity has its own city of PR articles.</p></li>
</ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>The state of Gen-Z’s gender divide in 2025.</strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Among Gen Z overall</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f37a6741-8add-4215-908a-64030cb7cfb6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">, 64% disapprove of Trump’s job performance</a><span> versus 36% who approve. But young men are more evenly split (53% disapprove, 47% approve) than young women (74% disapprove, 26% approve). The 21-point difference in Trump’s approval rating is unchanged from April.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>44% of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4c3d40b5-70cf-4120-ab83-645bfd0f97b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">UK Gen Z men</a><span> agree that ‘when it comes to giving women equal rights, things have gone far enough.’ While only 36% of 16-29-year old men agree that ‘feminism has done more good than harm’13, a concerning minority (20- 30%) feel threatened by female equality.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/84911209-030b-4d81-869f-8f0a89774c5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z </a><span>is undoubtedly diverging by gender, as 62% of single women report they are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/90f6efa1-da1a-4dbf-8a11-99594dac0279?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">not looking to date at all, compared with 37% of men</a><span>. And the gen Z women who are dating are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/73b16b2a-ce2c-4d7b-98c2-9c603769123b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">increasingly choosing older men</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>According to the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/78381f11-a2b2-4519-82e1-750907311934?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ipsos Global Trends</a><span> survey, 62% of men in this age group say that they "would like my country to be the way it used to be", compared to 50% of women in the same cohort.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gen Z is</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6e8fa867-a3a4-44a8-b0bd-c48bc813dd38?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> more progressive</a><span> than millennials, except in one crucial way.</span></p></li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ee4c7d51-8c7a-45ac-ab94-11cd1d8fe37e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></em></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Tracksuit and Bimma Williams present the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aedc603e-21e9-41e7-a67a-5fbc614e4897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">2025 Collab of the Year</a><span> Report</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>For the first time, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d0f8937a-9fdd-4968-9a58-ce2e380cec1f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bimma Williams</a><span> partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aedc603e-21e9-41e7-a67a-5fbc614e4897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">analyzed</a><span> the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy. And built the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aedc603e-21e9-41e7-a67a-5fbc614e4897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span>: an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy. </span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="890"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aedc603e-21e9-41e7-a67a-5fbc614e4897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/9b44d5a7-ff3f-445b-834e-bff80903e86b\_890x309.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":309,"width":890,"resizeWidth":null,"bytes":175878,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":"https://hubs.li/Q03XXxl30","belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/184011614?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9b44d5a7-ff3f-445b-834e-bff80903e86b\_890x309.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="190.95505617977528" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!3c09!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9b44d5a7-ff3f-445b-834e-bff80903e86b\_890x309.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aedc603e-21e9-41e7-a67a-5fbc614e4897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span> blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked </span><em>and</em><span> why:</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists.</p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aedc603e-21e9-41e7-a67a-5fbc614e4897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Collab of the Year</a><span> report.</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ol start="6" style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong><span>Gen Z is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a9e8955a-2264-472e-9e87-bf6d201d5139?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">afraid</a><span> of sex? </span></strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>While 46 percent of Gen Z respondents gave </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5aac6a3c-4be9-4fc3-89bf-c6a52d543d7c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a thumbs-up to sex scenes</a><span> with full nudity, nearly double that amount (90 percent) said they found emotional sex scenes enjoyable. In fact, 74 percent of Gen Z respondents said a sex scene doesn’t even need nudity to be considered a good watch.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9906c9c6-6102-4e47-927a-5a4b3310b910?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sex Recession</a><span>: The 27 to 43-year-olds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b9d920cd-721e-4656-93da-eb246981f8b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">topped the poll</a><span>, typically having sex 73 times a year, or 1.4 times a week. And last, but not least, is surprisingly Generation Z. The tech-savvy generation, thought to be aged 18 to 26, has sex 0.7 times a week.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Carter Sherman: “It is undeniably the case that they are having sex later and less frequently than previous generations. What is not true, from my reporting—and I talked to more than 100 people under 30 for the book—is that Gen Z isn't interested in sex. They're definitely not “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/42216581-a55f-4808-92c0-facadd3eff6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sex negative</a><span>.”</span></p></li>
</ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong><span>Gen-Z </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13ce892b-3f8a-4b30-8a91-d231ee8c77de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">doesn’t</a><span> drink alcohol…</span></strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>An IWSR survey of more than 26,000 people across the 15 largest alcoholic drinks markets found 73 per cent of Gen Z respondents — people of legal drinking age to 27 — had </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c04151ad-362a-4fe0-8e14-3f66bbabd182?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">consumed alcohol</a><span> in the previous six months, compared with 66 per cent two years ago.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Bartenders </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c68d67a7-f4b4-404a-9494-7e059b91fa94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">have a few complaints</a><span> about their younger customers: They don’t tip, they close their tab after every drink, and they nurse two beers all night. Of course, Gen Z has its own fair critiques: unaffordable housing, climate collapse, and late-stage capitalism.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span> About two-thirds of 18- to 34-year-olds believe moderate drinking is unhealthy, according to the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/412061d6-b915-48b5-ad19-724520f7d85e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">poll</a><span>, up from about 4 in 10 in 2015. Although </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4a42098-d2f8-435b-986c-3c30042d85f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">marijuana use is higher today</a><span> than a decade ago, it has been fairly steady over the past four years and thus doesn’t appear to be a factor in people choosing not to drink alcohol.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-roman;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>People who say Gen Z’s lack of drinking is causing them to socialise less are missing the point. So many zoomers are already drinking</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fa9c9285-62ee-42d5-a966-b53c5880a701?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> non-alcoholic beer</a><span> to socialise at bars. I mean, ask yourself, what is actually the point of non-alcoholic beer? They aren’t getting drunk while drinking something that tastes bitter. Zero benefits. They are doing it for fun and socialisation. It’s just like people having decaf coffee when they hang out late with friends. I find this obsession with making Gen Z drink to socialise a bit weird and outdated.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Also, the pattern of Gen Z not doing XYZ as much as millennials or Gen X isn’t limited to alcohol. From </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f806b51d-47cf-4520-8d29-3c1ec0e40f5f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">clubbing</a><span> to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/291d75cd-d283-4b79-97e5-186f4181fdc6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">walking</a><span>, this generation is more minimalist than all of the generations before.</span></p></li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>The faces are Gen-Z, trends are Gen Alpha.</strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Many trends in terms of consumption and creation are now driven by Gen Alpha’s interests, but those trends are still labeled as Gen Z.</p></li></ol>
</li>
</ol>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">2026 is 2016, at least when it comes to deciding when to post content.</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Back then, we used to overthink what time to post for the sake of engagement. Today, we need to overthink what time to post for the sake of sanity and brand safety. If your brand isn’t tracking the news cycle, is it even trying to build a brand on social media?</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Gen Z feeds are full of chaos, and yes, most people are only a couple of scrolls away from a mood change. But you can’t count on that. Something you can do is focus on mornings and sleepy time, or the seasons, and on some days, the afternoon. Now, what the hell am I talking about?</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Instagram Reels is filled with ragebait. You know that, I know that, and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a2ae29b2-927c-4b7e-8167-f0a7a9466f62?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">engagement farmer</a><span> in the comments know that. That’s the reason they comment, “Last reel before bed — the good ending.” Gen Z needs something to cheer up their mornings, but they won’t send GM messages like the uncs. That’s why Amazon Prime India has turned its content into a funny “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2560b600-d9d7-47ec-8084-cc3611cbad03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">good morning</a><span>” ritual. ESPN and many other brands have “XY of the day” content series to make sure something gets posted on their socials without being affected by the news cycle.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">I’m not advocating for brands to engage in escapism to divert attention from news that matters. Instead, my motive is to get brands to focus on emotions in 2026, to do something more ritualistic to cheer Gen Z and millennials when they feel low or when they are about to clock out in a bad mood.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">And to do that, you have to be the 2016 version of yourself, the one who hated the idea of posting at specific times because you said it was a gimmick. It’s kind of not a gimmick anymore, or even if it is, you have your existing audience ready to feel a positive emotion when you post early in the morning or during a late-night dump.</p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">The Zynners and Gen-Z’s gambling addiction</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>What happens when you combine the news cycle with actions that directly hinder the future of the current generation? You get a new economy of consumers </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f1de66a5-c15e-437c-ad7a-2b1d5dee7f83?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">gambling on everything</a><span>, because everything they do feels like a gamble. And they are the only ones experiencing it this way, as social media has once again shaped them like this.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Why are occasional photo dumps the new norm for Gen Z? Well, they used to post every now and then, but algorithms said nope, we’re not pushing this or that. So they stopped altogether and eliminated the risk by combining everything into one post. On the other hand, TikTok changed the way they consume content. YouTubers hyper-optimised their content to keep up with both the behavioural changes caused by TikTok and Instagram.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">The platforms transformed Gen Z’s state into escape-or-gamble mode, and once they reached adulthood, they started applying the same logic to the real world. And in a sense, why wouldn’t they? Layoffs, high rent, no community spaces, inflation, AI, and politics mean they’re facing a lot of challenges and don’t see a bright future.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Everyone is zoomed in on prediction markets, but everyone is taking a bet on something in one way or another. The gambling epidemic is less about what people bet on and more about what they skip. People are skipping long-term savings to have </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a3c3d81-2cb6-4eca-a355-16fb0111c3f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a moment of joy</a><span> today, betting on the moment because they don’t see the future. That’s the bigger picture. Here’s the other story, stat by stat:</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Gen Z’s awareness of vaping health hazards during COVID, and an increase in distribution led to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bb2caf3a-32f8-4629-b852-e688bf5d0d80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Zyn</a><span> breaking through the ceiling of mass culture. By the third quarter of 2024, Zyn accounted for 65.5 percent of US nicotine pouch sales by volume.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Zyn and the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f67c7b9f-dc75-4248-a1e8-33c31c4db822?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New Nicotine</a><span> Gold Rush. </span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Kidults: Circana found that kidults account for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d33fba7d-66e5-4e2a-bd15-98f23874855f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">28% of global toy sales</a><span>, an increase of 2.5% since 2022. Toy sales overall have struggled in recent years, but sales to kidults have gone up and to the right.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>China’s designer toy industry is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/55029bdf-47bb-425a-a725-f962c80f6fad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">projected</a><span> to reach more than 110 billion yuan, or US$15 billion, in retail sales in 2026—more than </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/39ea88cc-ea91-4225-ba85-0f5f84928e5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">1500% increase from 2015</a><span>, when retail sales were just US$890 million. Worldwide, the toy and figurine market is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/88fe1dd2-4211-4882-ab09-78beac5fa9c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">projected</a><span> to reach a market value of $49 billion by 2034, up from $26 billion last year.</span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>YPulse’s Generation Doom trend report shows 60% of 13-17-year-olds and 75% of 18-24-year-olds have </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7255f837-bb99-45bb-b87d-497afd4ccbca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an overall sense of doom</a><span> (i.e. a general feeling of pessimism or loss of hope) about the world and the future.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>1 in 5 Americans are ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/06f9107d-8b89-4ea3-9ab3-b0746bc7add9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">doom spending</a><span>’ — here’s how that can backfire.</span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>For Gen Z, ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a3c3d81-2cb6-4eca-a355-16fb0111c3f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Little Treats</a><span>’ Are Worth Going Over Budget: The last thing Naomi Barrales needed was a 30-minute train delay. She had a two-hour commute home to central New Jersey ahead of her. To kill time, she decided to treat herself to two vegan birthday cake cookies. It became a ritual.</span></p></li>
</ol>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">What if we are building trust the wrong way?</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>If you look at how Gen Z shows trust toward each other, the answer is very often through access and shareability. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/237ef204-8ac4-41f8-86c0-d1aa492399ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Location sharing</a><span> with friends is considered one of the bigger aspects of showing trust among teens. Meanwhile, the most adult groups are all in on close friends, and I feel like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f7d76d1c-6d85-49db-a187-47a791bbf5a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">every month</a><span> I read an article about a new relationship test to prove trust in each other. For years, we have considered password sharing a sign of trust. All roads lead to trust equaling the allowance of access to either yourself or what you own.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>As more and more people across the globe believe that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/18bd1d91-e20b-4aa8-8de8-ae774fd036f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">people in power</a><span> aren’t to be trusted, the numbers are only going to get even lower for brands. Advertising is already one of the least trusted industries, and we are dancing at the bottom of the table. In the last newsletter, I proposed how we can deal with this problem across demographics. For Gen Z, the answer is access. Give them access to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/33377329-ded0-4d1e-8759-c97274667309?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new spaces</a><span> and allow them to share your brand with others in an engaging way, and you will earn some of their access-based trust, the only available option.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="644"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2737944b-07a9-4444-85f1-01cd2f7780f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/81ade25f-3b54-44aa-bbdd-90db922c124c\_1220x780.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":780,"width":1220,"resizeWidth":644,"bytes":146748,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/184011614?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5d99e75b-5bf2-442e-9d7f-bf27ae349473\_1220x846.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="351.6393442622951" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!oQ88!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F81ade25f-3b54-44aa-bbdd-90db922c124c\_1220x780.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table>
<figcaption style="box-sizing: content-box;color: rgb(119,119,119);font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 400;letter-spacing: -.15px;margin-top: 8px;width: 70%;padding-left: 15%;padding-right: 15%;text-align: center;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f6b02031-eabd-488a-a30e-f64337514e25?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="text-decoration: underline;">Source</a></figcaption></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">What marketers often miss is that they want trust but don’t define the type. If you want to gain real trust, there is only one option, you have to show your pipeline and do the right thing. If you want internet trust, you have to play the game and jump on every trend that is considered authentic or rough. Most brands struggle to do both, so what they really want is a trade-off between trust and value, and that’s kind of what Gen Z can give brands.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>You give them something they can trust, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/acce9aaa-97bb-4844-b972-e9d5a5623b8d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">share, and access</a><span> with others, and you get back a level of brand trust or just money. Now, some brands and strategists have proposed “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/10c06b88-0ff9-41a8-ae97-c9cadb6d6331?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lore</a><span>” as the key to Gen Z’s trust and attention. Share the lore and win them over. In my view, this is a short-term solution to a long-term problem. Give them something better and more personal. Lore is a tactic; you need to build a strategy around access-based trust.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>I believe the success of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d2ee38a2-b260-451b-a1b4-c97258f0d566?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AMC’s A-List program</a><span> with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7383d0fb-b67c-4852-b446-47176807504e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z</a><span> is another great example of a brand combining an existing model of loyalty and experience programs with trust and community.</span></p>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">The Age of “Platformer” and Clipping Culture</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Not enough people ask this question: what are the biggest creators in the world actually bringing to the table themselves?</p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Podcasts: Joe Rogan’s own fans believe he no longer brings his interviewing skills to the table; the guests are running the show.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Streaming: Asmongold and Adin Ross are extremely problematic yet remain big in their space, and all they do is either have other people on their platforms or react to existing content. </p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">YouTube: MrBeast himself is the side story. Most of the time, he needs to bring in at least ten guests to make something work. Similarly, two of the most popular video formats of 2025 were 1 vs. 10 dating and Jubilee-style debates. The channels that hosted them were separate from the people starring in the content.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Substack:I won’t name names, but the majority of big newsletters are mostly doing interviews.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Instagram and TikTok: IG has been living in the age of the platformer forever, with content aggregators and theme pages ruling the platform, while TT also had its own age of the platformer, with Khaby Lame being the biggest creator.</p></li>
</ol>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">People platforming others at scale are ruling the media economy. The money is in platforming people, everything else is too expensive or doesn’t confirm distribution. At least that’s what Gen Z and Gen Alpha have proved so far. They continue to push creators who lack talent themselves but curate people or information in bulk to keep viewers engaged.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Then, creators lure in audiences through clipping and social media edits meant to funnel the audience into the main content. Whether it’s aura-farming clips of some Twitch streamers or a hyper-edited teaser for the Steven Bartlett podcast.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Gen Z basically wants the package deal, and honestly, at this moment, the people providing that deal couldn’t be worse. Brands like </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/579db841-9558-4b3c-8af1-fc0fe5a4d1c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lowe’s</a><span> and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/08312337-380a-43c9-bb2f-bd3b3733df73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Dick’s Sporting Goods</a><span> trying to build creator networks should focus more on establishing a platform or package instead of using influencers/creators for clout.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">READ! OR READ?</p></blockquote>
<div data-component-name="DigestPostEmbedStatic" style="margin-bottom: 20px;padding: 0;position: relative;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" role="presentation" width="auto"><tbody>
<tr><td><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/20440171-f9f1-430c-b933-1aa0f4cc540f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" style="text-decoration: none;"><h2 style="list-style: none;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 700;margin: 0;color: #363737;line-height: 36px;font-size: 30px;text-decoration: unset;">I wouldn't market to Gen-Z in 2025 without knowing these stats and insights</h2></a></td></tr>

<tr><td><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/20440171-f9f1-430c-b933-1aa0f4cc540f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" style="text-decoration: none;"><p style="text-decoration: unset;list-style: none;margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Many insights I shared in the 2025 version of this post are still valid. That’s why I highly recommend a re-read of last year’s newsletter.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"></p></a></td></tr>

</tbody></table></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">
<span>Why everything is ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7709e1e8-e666-4ce1-b3c0-326ccf35540d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">aesthetic</a><span>’ to Gen Z and Alpha.</span>
</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">SPEED. SLOP. SHUT. </p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Everything moves at a high f\*\*king speed, so they don’t meaningfully consume anything. Once they do develop an interest, the creators they follow sooner or later fall into the trap of being a creator and start producing slop. There’s a reason the most reposted meme on r/YouTube is about audiences getting over their favourite YouTubers. And once they grow old in this loop of slop and speed, they shut themselves off from long-term ideas and start treating everything like an aesthetic.</p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="674"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c67d9399-70bd-477f-8069-d3c9dcdd0f6b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/fe536020-2597-4ff7-a956-57989897f8f0\_2386x1565.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":955,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":674,"bytes":1559596,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/184011614?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffe536020-2597-4ff7-a956-57989897f8f0\_2386x1565.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="360.7486263736264" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!lRAw!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Ffe536020-2597-4ff7-a956-57989897f8f0\_2386x1565.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">And yes, everything is an aesthetic to a generation that grew up on anti-link social media. Millennials and Gen X lived through the era of blogging and websites, where your interests led you somewhere. Gen Z and Gen Alpha are so disconnected that they confuse most Tumblr aesthetics with “2016” every now and then. They say “that’s so aesthetic” about everything because they lack cultural context. Tumblr gave rise to the word “aesthetic,” but it was also informing people with links to the origins of that aesthetic. Today, the story is that there are no links, and most zoomers, like other users, don’t take action to search the aesthetics. Even if they do, you know it only takes ten scrolls to reach sexualised content on TikTok.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Gen Z and Gen Alpha declare aesthetics without much documentation or preservation of those aesthetics. They aren’t at fault, the platforms that don’t allow this are. Not all bad news, though, as marketers and cultural analysts at brands work overtime documenting everything both generations do.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>I may have gone a little far from why this matters to you. The short answer is this: if everything non-branded and of more value can be considered just an aesthetic, where does your brand land? Don’t be too harsh about promoting the web or offline experiences of your brand, it’s only for the best. It’s time we move past infotainment on social media and do something on the web, and the web </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99a4b067-fc31-41a1-a1f0-2ee0269ea40c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">needs</a><span> your help.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">P.S. Comments like “Why isn’t anyone talking about this?” and “what about my XYZ?” are a great example of how the lack of clear paths forward on websites makes Gen Z and Gen Alpha more prone to questioning rather than seeking answers.</p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>

<tr><td><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8adb8bd3-1da7-4d57-95a1-d4da098ff013?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" style="text-decoration: none;"><p style="text-decoration: unset;list-style: none;margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">This newsletter, which I wrote with Mara Dettmann, covers everything else we know about generations and culture. </p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"></p></a></td></tr>

<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">My Generational Reads of the year</h3>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>I’m a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a75c4efc-ed39-4223-82c6-ca38b912221f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z Journalist</a><span>. My Generation Doesn’t Know What That Means. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The midlife crisis is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3ab45e74-a5da-4b73-ac50-c866eeafc0f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">coming</a><span> for millennials lol.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c660fc0f-0750-4a2c-90a7-66090f804bae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Unemployment</a><span> by degrees. </span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gen Z’s hole in the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7dcd2a1c-e8a8-4940-b542-e5d1ef87f823?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">labor market</a><span> could soon grow.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>College graduates are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2ffb2897-a0cf-4def-8c74-d3b2449f9b59?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">trading</a><span> desk jobs for blue-collar work.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Ski Industry is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/67c2963f-77ae-46e6-99aa-f6d902e043a1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ignoring</a><span> Gen Z.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/134034f8-d6a6-4eff-a9e4-42b0e07297eb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen X clubbers</a><span> party like it’s the 90s but are home by 9pm.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Too Many Trends! - The NY Times </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea5eaf78-c4cf-462d-8fc8-d1f5f466a7b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">asked</a><span> Gen Zers about the impact of constantly changing and recurring trends on their lives.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>We asked </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b5206c6b-753d-4843-9463-f058a2a613c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">young people</a><span> what would actually make them happy.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>What’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d0da85a5-c3bb-4c98-95dc-2ece92c1e42e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">going on</a><span> with men?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gen Z Only </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/39436cf6-ee9e-4a94-8905-05fe8b93f861?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Watches TV</a><span> Through Social Clips. Hollywood is Scrambling.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Joy of Gen Z’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/367c8440-64d7-4e67-a2a8-d0b971b3d2f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">New Perfume Culture</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gen Z is the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7eb0a3f6-b00c-4849-a1c0-6b404c3b302f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> most rejected</a><span> generation: College, Dating, and Jobs.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Covid created two very </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/252302db-c79b-40b6-95a7-8d964e95e9dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">divided cohorts</a><span> of Gen Z - WaPo x </span><a href="https://open.substack.com/users/3404063-rachel-janfaza?utm\_source=mentions" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Rachel Janfaza</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Young women are starting to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/298c3523-ceda-477a-b46e-33f6e123b6be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">recession-proof</a><span> their lives. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8fe1a905-3572-47f4-8a3c-8855d82fc013?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Gen Z Lifestyle Subsidy</a><span> — In the 2010s, Millennials got cheap Ubers. Today’s young people are getting free SuperGrok.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Plants, skincare, oat milk: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f41207a3-2260-4224-a3d2-eab735743c19?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z</a><span> are the subscription kings.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Young people can’t buy homes, but they can </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/582d265f-e3a2-4dff-b168-9c4ce6d4ce81?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">generate</a><span> intellectual property.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc1acda6-148e-4e51-abee-a05530025c6a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Kid rotting</a><span>’: why parents are letting their children go wild this summer.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why is Gen Z getting more </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/93e29474-13f6-4e43-a1ca-213298ce18de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">religious</a><span>? We asked them.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Against the odds, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b71f92b9-c385-4c78-9d8d-832893a65af6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z</a><span> is breaking into the housing market.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Knowledgemaxxing, Research as a Leisure Activity and the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b5f43fb-c76d-4e03-b48a-0744cf5d5eac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z Yearning</a><span> for Intellectual Growth.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a59b2283-5119-4e44-9666-80d825fc6d54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Zillennials</a><span> are taking over.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The inside story of how </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2afd2504-4111-47c1-b833-cb240d686992?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z toppled Nepal’s leader</a><span> and chose a new one on Discord.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/87100ef3-439d-4831-ad4b-423602dc1458?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">It’s PR, not the ER</a><span>’: Gen Z is resisting the workplace emergency.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Trad Wives, Move Over. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/660ee062-8097-48e4-ab73-cda63e0e1e2b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Trad Sons</a><span> Are the New Homesteading Influencers.</span></p></li>
